Wage Type Catalogue!P1	Special Tax (Non-resident flat 20% on gross, ET art. 408)
Wage Type Catalogue!Q1	Subject to Health - Salud EPS (EE 4% + ER 8.5%; IBC, cap 25 SMMLV 43,772,625)
Wage Type Catalogue!R1	Subject to Solidarity Pension Fund - FSP (EE 1% + 0.2-1.0%; IBC >= 4 SMMLV 7,003,620)
Wage Type Catalogue!S1	Subject to Occupational Risk - ARL (ER only 0.348-8.7%; IBC)
Wage Type Catalogue!T1	Subject to Payroll Levies - CCF 4% / SENA 2% / ICBF 3% (Ley 21/1982 base, NO ceiling)
Wage Type Catalogue!U1	Subject to Pension - Pension Obligatoria (EE 4% + ER 12%; IBC, cap 25 SMMLV)
Wage Type Catalogue!V1	Salary or Non-Salary (CST arts. 127 / 128) - hard flag for the nomina electronica S / NS elements. Non-Salary is split for the Ley 1393 de 2010 art. 30 40% clamp: 'Non-Salary - pacted' = a payment that would remunerate the service but was excluded from salary by an express CST art. 128 pact (or a collective agreement) - INSIDE the 40% test; 'Non-Salary - non-remunerative' = non-salary by law or by nature (does not remunerate the service: transport allowance, occasional per diem, means and tools to do the job, dotacion, payments by mere liberality, indemnities, social-security benefits) - OUTSIDE the 40% test on both sides: not in the numerator (Consejo de Estado, Seccion Cuarta, sentencia de unificacion rad. 05001-23-33-000-2016-02496-01 (25185), 9 December 2021, rule 3) and not in the total remuneration (UGPP, ABC Empleadores 2025: total remunerado = all salary payments + pacted non-salary payments + paid leave). Where the nature is unclear the row is 'pacted' and the DD non-salary pact flag decides. Both values map to the nomina electronica NS element.

Employer Sick Pay!A1	COLOMBIA - EMPLOYER SICK PAY AND THE INCAPACITY CASCADE (2026)
Employer Sick Pay!A3	Colombia has NO employer sick-pay scheme of the Czech kind. Cash sickness benefit is a social-security
Employer Sick Pay!A4	benefit, and the employer bears only the FIRST TWO DAYS of a general-illness incapacity.
Employer Sick Pay!A6	WHO PAYS WHAT
Employer Sick Pay!A7	  General illness, days 1-2 .......... EMPLOYER          66.67% (Mercans default, configurable)
Employer Sick Pay!A8	  General illness, days 3-90 ......... EPS (health insurer)   66.67% of the contribution base
Employer Sick Pay!A9	  General illness, days 91-180 ....... EPS                    50.00% of the contribution base
Employer Sick Pay!A10	  General illness, days 181-540 ...... AFP (pension admin.)   50.00% - 'subsidio equivalente' (Ley 100 art. 41);
Employer Sick Pay!A11	                                       the EPS pays if no rehabilitation concept was issued before day 120
Employer Sick Pay!A12	  General illness, day 541 onward .... EPS (ADRES-funded)     amount recognised by the EPS (Decreto 780 art. 2.2.3.4.3)
Employer Sick Pay!A13	  Occupational accident or disease ... ARL (risk insurer)    100.00% of the contribution base,
Employer Sick Pay!A14	                                                            from the DAY AFTER the event
Employer Sick Pay!A15	  Maternity leave, 18 weeks .......... EPS   salary earned when the leave begins
Employer Sick Pay!A16	  Paternity leave, 2 weeks ........... EPS   full salary (expands 1 week per point of structural
Employer Sick Pay!A17	                                             unemployment reduction, capped at 5 weeks)
Employer Sick Pay!A19	FORMULAE
Employer Sick Pay!A20	  Employer days 1-2 ............. S / 30 x 2/3 x d, floored at the minimum-wage equivalent
Employer Sick Pay!A21	  EPS days 3-90 ................. IBC / 30 x 2/3 x d, floored at the minimum-wage equivalent
Employer Sick Pay!A22	  EPS days 91-180 ............... IBC / 30 x 0.5000 x d, floored at the minimum-wage equivalent
Employer Sick Pay!A23	  AFP days 181-540 .............. IBC / 30 x 0.5000 x d; IBC of the month before the INITIAL incapacity
Employer Sick Pay!A24	  ARL, occupational ............. IBC / 30 x 1.0000 x d
Employer Sick Pay!A25	  Maternity ..................... S / 30 x 126 calendar days
Employer Sick Pay!A26	  Paternity ..................... S / 30 x 14 calendar days
Employer Sick Pay!A27	  Variable salary ............... average of the year of service preceding the incapacity, or of the
Employer Sick Pay!A28	                                  whole period if under a year (CST art. 228)
Employer Sick Pay!A30	OPEN ITEM 1 - THE RATE FOR THE EMPLOYER'S FIRST TWO DAYS.
Employer Sick Pay!A31	  Decreto 2943 de 2013 art. 1, compiled at Decreto 780 de 2016 art. 3.2.1.10, allocates 'las
Employer Sick Pay!A32	  prestaciones economicas correspondientes a los dos (2) primeros dias de incapacidad originada por
Employer Sick Pay!A33	  enfermedad general' to the EMPLOYER but does NOT state the rate. CST art. 227 sets the auxilio at two
Employer Sick Pay!A34	  thirds for the first 90 days, and market practice applies 66.67% for days 1-2 on that basis; a
Employer Sick Pay!A35	  minority practice pays 100%. MERCANS DEFAULT: 66.67%, configurable. Carried in the QA report.
Employer Sick Pay!A37	OPEN ITEM 2 - THE MINIMUM-WAGE FLOOR ON INCAPACITY PAYMENTS.
Employer Sick Pay!A38	  Where 66.67% of salary falls below one SMMLV (1,750,905), market practice and settled
Employer Sick Pay!A39	  administrative doctrine top the payment up to one minimum wage. The primary regulatory source for
Employer Sick Pay!A40	  that floor was not retrieved this pass. MERCANS DEFAULT: apply the floor, configurable. Carried in
Employer Sick Pay!A41	  the QA report.
Employer Sick Pay!A43	PAYROLL TREATMENT (the counter-intuitive part)
Employer Sick Pay!A44	  Employer days 1-2 ....... NOT salary, but IN the IBC; NOT in the Ley 21/1982 payroll-levy base (art. 17) and NOT in
Employer Sick Pay!A45	                            the statutory-benefit base (the days count, the amount does not); TAXABLE.
Employer Sick Pay!A46	  EPS / ARL benefits ...... NOT employer salary, so OUT of the payroll-levy base - but CONTRIBUTIONS
Employer Sick Pay!A47	                            CONTINUE ON THE BASE (PILA change events IGE, IRL, LMA). TAXABLE -
Employer Sick Pay!A48	                            not exempt income: the benefits are not indemnities under ET art. 206
Employer Sick Pay!A49	                            nums. 1-2 (DIAN Oficio 27408 de 2019 items 7.1-7.4). Advanced by the employer
Employer Sick Pay!A50	                            through payroll and recovered from the insurer; the PILA carries the authorisation
Employer Sick Pay!A51	                            number and value (T2R2-057 to T2R2-060) so the recovery can be offset.
Employer Sick Pay!A52	  Auxilio de transporte ... NOT PAYABLE for sick days - no travel occurs.
Employer Sick Pay!A53	  Overtime and premia ..... Not applicable during incapacity. No ARL contribution for incapacity days (Decreto 1072 art. 2.2.4.2.1.6).
Employer Sick Pay!A54	  Benefit accrual ......... Continues: incapacity days count for prima, cesantias and vacation, but the
Employer Sick Pay!A55	                            incapacity amount is left out of the salary average (CSJ SL1659-2025); SUSPENDED only for unpaid leave (CST art. 53).
Employer Sick Pay!A57	Sources: CST arts. 227, 228, 236, 239; Decreto 2943 de 2013 art. 1; Decreto 780 de 2016 art. 3.2.1.10;
Employer Sick Pay!A58	ET art. 206 nums. 1-2 and par. 1; DIAN Oficio 27408 de 2019; Ley 100 de 1993 art. 41; Ley 1753 de 2015 art. 67; Decreto 2126 de 2023; Decreto 1072 de 2015 art. 2.2.4.2.1.6; Ley 361 de 1997 art. 26; Resolucion MSPS 2388 de 2016 Anexo Tecnico 2;
Employer Sick Pay!A59	Resolucion DIAN 000227 de 2025 arts. 1.5.3.1.1 to 1.5.3.9.8, Anexo Tecnico v1.0.
Tax Credit!A1	COLOMBIA - TAX CREDITS: NOT APPLICABLE. THE EQUIVALENT MECHANISMS ARE DEDUCTIONS AND EXEMPT INCOME.
Tax Credit!A3	Colombia has NO tax credit against the payroll withholding - no personal credit, no child credit, no
Tax Credit!A4	disability credit and no non-refundable family credit of the kind the CZ template contemplates. Relief
Tax Credit!A5	is delivered instead by SUBTRACTING amounts from the base, in THREE legally distinct classes whose
Tax Credit!A6	class determines where each item sits in the ET art. 388 depuration and which caps it consumes.
Tax Credit!A7	Getting the class wrong is the most common source of withholding error in Colombia.
Tax Credit!A9	UVT (tax unit) 2026 = COP 52,374   (Resolucion DIAN 000238 de 15 diciembre 2025 art. 1; the 2026
Tax Credit!A10	uprating was certified at 5.17%, taking the unit from COP 49,799). Every tax parameter is indexed to
Tax Credit!A11	the UVT and NO tax threshold is expressed in minimum wages - that is the one-caliber discipline:
Tax Credit!A12	minimum wage for social security and labour, UVT for tax. The two are never mixed.
Tax Credit!A14	CLASS A - NON-TAXABLE INCOME (ingresos no constitutivos de renta ni ganancia ocasional)
Tax Credit!A15	  Subtracted at step (B), BEFORE the 40% cap reference figure is struck, so it does NOT consume the cap.
Tax Credit!A16	  A1  Mandatory pension contribution ......... 4% of the IBC .................... ET art. 55
Tax Credit!A17	  A2  Solidarity pension fund ............... 1% + 0.2-1.0% of the IBC .......... ET art. 55
Tax Credit!A18	  A3  Mandatory health contribution ......... 4% of the IBC .................... ET art. 56
Tax Credit!A19	  A4  Voluntary contributions to the individual pension account (RAIS):
Tax Credit!A20	        the lower of 25% of annual labour income and 2,500 UVT = COP 130,935,000 . ET art. 55
Tax Credit!A21	        (a non-qualifying withdrawal is withheld at 35%)
Tax Credit!A22	  A5  Third-party meal payments: not income up to 41 UVT/month = 2,147,334,
Tax Credit!A23	        where the employee's salary does not exceed 310 UVT = 16,235,940 ..... ET art. 387-1
Tax Credit!A25	CLASS B - DEDUCTIONS AND EXEMPT INCOME (deducciones y rentas exentas)
Tax Credit!A26	  Subtracted at steps (D) and (E), INSIDE the 40% and 1,340-UVT caps.
Tax Credit!A27	  B1  Housing-loan interest and monetary correction ... 100 UVT/month = COP 5,237,400 . ET arts. 119, 387
Tax Credit!A28	  B2  Prepaid medicine / health insurance ............  16 UVT/month = COP   837,984 . ET art. 387 lits. a, b
Tax Credit!A29	        (the provider must be supervised by the National Health Superintendence or the
Tax Credit!A30	         Financial Superintendence)
Tax Credit!A31	  B3  Dependants: 10% of the month's gross labour income, capped 32 UVT = COP 1,675,968 . ET art. 387
Tax Credit!A32	        NOTE: 10% OF GROSS REGARDLESS OF THE NUMBER OF DEPENDANTS - it does not multiply.
Tax Credit!A33	  B4  Voluntary pension funds + AFC / AVC long-term savings: the lower of 30% of labour
Tax Credit!A34	        income and 3,800 UVT/year = COP 199,021,200, SHARED between the two vehicles . ET arts. 126-1, 126-4
Tax Credit!A35	  B5  THE 25% EXEMPT-INCOME DEDUCTION ................ 790 UVT/year = COP 41,375,460 . ET art. 206 num. 10
Tax Credit!A36	        COMPUTED LAST, on the figure net of non-taxable income, the art. 387 deductions and all other
Tax Credit!A37	        exempt income: 'El calculo de esta renta exenta se efectuara una vez se detraiga del valor
Tax Credit!A38	        total de los pagos laborales recibidos por el trabajador, los ingresos no constitutivos de
Tax Credit!A39	        renta, las deducciones y las demas rentas exentas diferentes a la establecida en el presente
Tax Credit!A40	        numeral.' Computing 25% of GROSS overstates it and under-withholds.
Tax Credit!A41	        Monthly apportionment used: 65.8333 UVT = 3,447,955.
Tax Credit!A42	  B6  Cesantias and their interest: exempt in full where the average monthly income of the last six
Tax Credit!A43	        months is <= 350 UVT = 18,330,900; then tapering 90 / 80 / 60 / 40 / 20 / 0%
Tax Credit!A44	        through 410 / 470 / 530 / 590 / 650 UVT ......................... ET art. 206 num. 4
Tax Credit!A45	        They are EXCLUDED from the monthly base entirely under both procedures, and the 25%
Tax Credit!A46	        exemption does NOT apply to them (art. 206 par. 2).
Tax Credit!A47	  B7  Pension income: taxable only above 1,000 UVT/month = COP 52,374,000 ...... ET art. 206 num. 5
Tax Credit!A48	  B8  Accident, illness and maternity-protection indemnities and burial expenses: exempt ONLY up to
Tax Credit!A49	        the statutory minimum amount ................................. ET art. 206 nums. 1-3, par. 1
Tax Credit!A51	CLASS C - OUTSIDE THE CAPS ENTIRELY
Tax Credit!A52	  C1  Additional dependants deduction: 72 UVT per dependant per year = COP 3,770,928 each,
Tax Credit!A53	        maximum 4 dependants = COP 15,083,712/year, i.e. 6 UVT = COP 314,244 per dependant
Tax Credit!A54	        per month ..................................... ET art. 336 num. 3, second paragraph
Tax Credit!A55	        'en adicion al limite establecido en el inciso anterior' - it is applied AFTER and OUTSIDE
Tax Credit!A56	        the 40% and 1,340-UVT caps.
Tax Credit!A57	  STACKING: an EMPLOYEE may take BOTH B3 and C1 FOR THE SAME DEPENDANT. Decreto 2231 de 2023 art. 3:
Tax Credit!A58	        'el mismo dependiente solo dara lugar a una de estas dos deducciones, excepto cuando el
Tax Credit!A59	        contribuyente tenga ingresos por rentas de trabajo provenientes de una relacion laboral o
Tax Credit!A60	        legal y reglamentaria, en cuyo caso podran aplicarse ambas deducciones por el mismo
Tax Credit!A61	        dependiente.'
Tax Credit!A63	THE COMBINED CAP
Tax Credit!A64	  (D + E) <= 40% of (C = A - B)   AND   (D + E) <= 1,340 UVT/year = COP 70,181,160
Tax Credit!A65	  Monthly apportionment used: 111.6667 UVT = 5,848,430.
Tax Credit!A66	  OPEN ITEM: the 790-UVT and 1,340-UVT caps are stated ANNUALLY yet withholding is monthly. Market
Tax Credit!A67	  practice, which Mercans follows, apportions them at one twelfth and tests each month against the
Tax Credit!A68	  apportioned figure. No regulation prescribes the apportionment expressly. Carried in the QA report.
Tax Credit!A69	  The CURRENT year's UVT applies to the annual caps (DIAN Concepto 015007 int. 973 de 13 septiembre 2023).
Tax Credit!A71	NOT AVAILABLE AGAINST WITHHOLDING
Tax Credit!A72	  The 1% / 240-UVT invoice-based deduction of ET art. 336 num. 5 is expressly excluded - 'no se tendra
Tax Credit!A73	  en cuenta para el calculo de la retencion en la fuente' - confirmed by DIAN Concepto 015007 int. 973
Tax Credit!A74	  de 13 septiembre 2023. It is available only in the employee's own annual return.
Tax Credit!A76	NOT A CREDIT, AND NOT A TAX
Tax Credit!A77	  The Contribucion Solidaria a la Educacion Superior (Contribucion Sabes) is a CST art. 150 permitted
Tax Credit!A78	  DEDUCTION added by Ley 1911 de 2018 art. 22, not a tax and not a credit - it must never be
Tax Credit!A79	  aggregated with the retencion en la fuente on Formulario 350.
Tax Credit!A81	DECLARATIONS THE EMPLOYEE MUST MAKE (no relief is automatic except the 25%)
Tax Credit!A82	  Housing-loan interest ... lender certificate.  Prepaid health ... provider certificate.
Tax Credit!A83	  Dependants .............. number, EACH DEPENDANT'S IDENTITY DOCUMENT, relationship and basis of
Tax Credit!A84	                            dependency; for a dependent spouse, parent or sibling, a public
Tax Credit!A85	                            accountant's certificate of annual income below 260 UVT (COP 13,617,240)
Tax Credit!A86	                            or a certified state of physical or psychological dependence.
Tax Credit!A87	  Voluntary pension / AFC / AVC / RAIS ... fund or administrator certificates.
Tax Credit!A88	  Higher withholding rate ... written request, effective the month AFTER it is made (ET art. 383 par. 3).
Tax Credit!A89	  WHO QUALIFIES AS A DEPENDANT (ET art. 387 par. 2 nums. 2-3 as amended by Ley 2411 de 2024 art. 2):
Tax Credit!A90	    children up to 18; children 18-25 whose higher or technical education the taxpayer finances;
Tax Credit!A91	    children over 18 in a certified state of dependence; a spouse or permanent partner dependent by
Tax Credit!A92	    absence of income or with annual income below 260 UVT; parents and siblings on the same conditions.
Tax Credit!A94	OUT OF MERCANS SCOPE BUT FLAGGED SO THE ENGINE DOES NOT SILENTLY CLAMP THEM
Tax Credit!A95	  ET art. 206 par. 4 and Decreto 1625 de 2016 art. 1.2.4.1.6 par. 3 place art. 206 nums. 6-9, the
Tax Credit!A96	  prima especial and the prima de costo de vida OUTSIDE the 40% and 1,340-UVT limits. These attach to
Tax Credit!A97	  military, judicial, diplomatic and public university staff - out of private-sector scope.
Tax Credit!A99	NON-RESIDENTS GET NONE OF THE ABOVE
Tax Credit!A100	  ET art. 408: a flat 20% on the GROSS payment, with no depuration, no art. 387 deductions, no 25%
Tax Credit!A101	  exempt income and no tax-free band. DIAN Concepto 011108 de 29 junio 2026.

Global WTC Coverage!A6	5.0
Global WTC Coverage!B6	Colombia-specific additions
Global WTC Coverage!C6	102 CO-prefixed wage types: 55 earnings (core pay, the six premium hour types, the statutory benefits, the leave cascade, the termination heads), 19 employee deductions, 11 employer contributions and accruals, 17 bases / intermediaries / rates / flags.
Global WTC Coverage!A7	6.0
Global WTC Coverage!B7	Total rows in the Wage Type Catalogue
Global WTC Coverage!C7	402
Global WTC Coverage!A8	7.0
Global WTC Coverage!B8	SS-fund column expansion (why five, not one)
Global WTC Coverage!C8	Colombia has EIGHT mandatory contributions across THREE insurance branches, THREE payroll levies and ONE employee-only solidarity surcharge, and they do NOT share a base. Q1 = Salud (EE 4% + ER 8.5%, IBC, cap 25 SMMLV, ER exonerable). Q2 = Fondo de Solidaridad Pensional (EE only, 1% from 4 SMMLV plus 0.2-1.0% from 16 SMMLV, IBC). Q3 = Riesgos Laborales / ARL (ER only, 0.348-8.7% by work centre, IBC). Q4 = Parafiscales CCF 4% + SENA 2% + ICBF 3% on the LEY 21 DE 1982 ART. 17 BASE WITH NO CEILING. R = Pension (EE 4% + ER 12%, IBC, cap 25 SMMLV, never exonerable). Collapsing Q4 into the others would silently cap the levies at 25 SMMLV and would drop paid rest days from their base.
Global WTC Coverage!A9	8.0
Global WTC Coverage!B9	Three Colombia-specific base columns added (S, T, U)
Global WTC Coverage!C9	S = the HARD CST art. 127 / 128 salary flag, which the nomina electronica demands at ELEMENT LEVEL through paired S / NS element names and which must never be derived at reporting time. T = the statutory-benefit base (prima / cesantias / intereses), which INCLUDES the auxilio de transporte even though the IBC and the levy base exclude it. U = the vacation base of CST art. 192, which excludes ONLY rest-day work and supplementary overtime hours, so the night premium and commissions stay in. Values: Salary / Non-Salary / Paid Rest / Conditional / NA for S; Yes / No / Yes-No / NA for T and U.
Global WTC Coverage!A10	9.0
Global WTC Coverage!B10	Column meaning adopted (N, O, P)
Global WTC Coverage!C10	N = subject to retencion en la fuente at all. O = subject to the ET art. 383 progressive table (0 / 19 / 28 / 33 / 35 / 37 / 39% on the art. 388 depurated base). P = SPECIAL TAX is used for the NON-RESIDENT flat 20% on gross of ET art. 408 - the treatment that produces a 17-fold withholding difference on the same salary and is the single largest configuration risk in Colombian expatriate payroll.
Global WTC Coverage!A11	10.0
Global WTC Coverage!B11	Yes-No entries and their justification
Global WTC Coverage!C11	Every Yes-No (rendered 'Yes/No') marks a genuine statutory conditionality, and column D carries the explanation in every case, as the v2.5 C3 guard requires. The recurring reasons are: (a) the LEY 1393/2010 ART. 30 40% CLAMP, which excludes a validly pacted non-salary payment from the IBC only up to 40% of total remuneration and forces the excess back in; (b) the ET art. 387-1 meal thresholds; (c) the ET art. 206 num. 4 cesantias taper; (d) the ET art. 206 par. 1 statutory-minimum limit on EPS / ARL benefit exemptions; (e) the apprentice's phase-dependent regime; (f) the CST art. 130 viaticos split; and (g) the conditional character of an anticipo.
Global WTC Coverage!A12	11.0
Global WTC Coverage!B12	2026 parameters used, and their verification
Global WTC Coverage!C12	SMMLV 1,750,905 (Decreto 1469 de 2025 art. 1 - VERIFIED in the source PDF, which also records that the Council of State suspended the decree on 12 February 2026, that Decreto 0159 de 2026 re-fixed the identical amount, and that the suspension was revoked on 9 July 2026, so the AMOUNT was never in dispute). UVT 52,374 (Resolucion DIAN 000238 de 15-12-2025 art. 1 - VERIFIED in the source PDF). Auxilio de transporte 249,095 (Decreto 1470 de 2025 art. 1 - the decree itself is not in 00-official-sources; the figure is carried from CO-CCG-001 and is corroborated by the exact COP 2,000,000 combined minimum cash entitlement). IBC ceiling 25 SMMLV = 43,772,625 and salario integral at 70% (Ley 797 de 2003 art. 5 - VERIFIED verbatim). 40% clamp (Ley 1393 de 2010 art. 30 - VERIFIED verbatim). Levy base including paid rest days (Ley 21 de 1982 art. 17 - VERIFIED verbatim). Rest-day premium 80% -> 90% on 1 July 2026 (Ley 2466 de 2025 art. 14 - VERIFIED verbatim). Night window 19:00-06:00 (Ley 2466 art. 10 - VERIFIED verbatim). 42-hour week (Ley 2466 art. 11 - VERIFIED verbatim). Salary in kind 50% / 30% (CST art. 129 - VERIFIED verbatim). Vacation base (CST art. 192 - VERIFIED verbatim). Viaticos (CST art. 130 - VERIFIED verbatim). Tips (CST art. 131 - VERIFIED verbatim). Dotacion every four months (CST arts. 230, 232 - VERIFIED verbatim). Apprentice 75% / 100% (Ley 2466 art. 21 - VERIFIED verbatim). Connectivity allowance (Ley 2466 art. 53 - VERIFIED verbatim). Cesantias 8.33% monthly advance (Ley 2466 art. 64 - VERIFIED verbatim).
Global WTC Coverage!A13	12.0
Global WTC Coverage!B13	Newest-law check (control C2)
Global WTC Coverage!C13	LEY 2381 DE 2024, the complete replacement of the Colombian pension architecture, is the newest pension law on the statute book and it does NOT govern any 2026 payroll period. Its own art. 94 set entry into force at 1 July 2025; Constitutional Court Auto 841 de 18 junio 2025 SUSPENDED that entry into force; and Judgment C-264 de 25 agosto 2026 upheld art. 94 on the understanding that the upheld provisions take effect on 1 APRIL 2027. Every 2026 period therefore runs on Ley 100 de 1993 as amended by Ley 797 de 2003, and NO part of Ley 2381 is configured as live. What changes in 2027: only the solidarity-fund surcharge, which becomes a single five-band 1.5% / 1.8% / 2.5% / 2.8% / 3.0% scale - the 16% total, the 12 / 4 split and the 25-SMMLV ceiling are unchanged. Recorded as a 2027 change-control item.
Global WTC Coverage!A14	13.0
Global WTC Coverage!B14	Configuration source
Global WTC Coverage!C14	Configured fresh for Colombia from CO-CCG-001 v1.0 Modules 3-10, the 04-reports-spec files, and the primary law in 00-official-sources/pdfs/ (CST Decreto 2663 de 1950, Ley 100 de 1993, Ley 797 de 2003, Ley 1122 de 2007, Ley 1393 de 2010, Ley 21 de 1982, Ley 89 de 1988, Ley 2101 de 2021, Ley 2381 de 2024, Ley 2466 de 2025, Decreto 1469 de 2025, Decreto 0223 de 2026, Decreto 1990 de 2016, Decreto Ley 624 de 1989, Decreto Ley 2090 de 2003, Resoluciones DIAN 000227 y 000238 de 2025, Resolucion 2388 de 2016 y Anexo 2, Circular MinTrabajo 0101 de 2025, Oficio DIAN 13840 de 2025). NOT copied from Czechia, Cote d'Ivoire or any other country.
Global WTC Coverage!A15	14.0
Global WTC Coverage!B15	DERIVED flags
Global WTC Coverage!C15	(1) The Mercans code numbers for the 102 Colombia-specific rows are DERIVED - no Mercans numeric code range has been assigned for Colombia, so mnemonic CO- codes are used and must be renumbered at system configuration. (2) The auxilio de transporte amount is carried from the CCG because Decreto 1470 de 2025 is not in 00-official-sources. (3) The additional high-risk pension rate is read from the Resolucion 2388 rate table (26% total against 16% standard, implying +10 pp employer-borne), NOT from the implementing decree - see the open items below.
Global WTC Coverage!A16	15.0
Global WTC Coverage!B16	Open items carried to the QA report
Global WTC Coverage!C16	(1) The rate for the employer's first two sick days - Decreto 780 de 2016 art. 3.2.1.10 allocates the days but not the rate; Mercans default 66.67%, configurable. (2) The minimum-wage floor on incapacity payments - primary source not retrieved; Mercans default applies the floor, configurable. (3) The additional employer pension contribution for Decreto Ley 2090 de 2003 high-risk activities - the 2026 rate and base not confirmed from a primary regulatory source. (4) The monthly apportionment of the annual 790-UVT and 1,340-UVT tax caps - no regulation prescribes it expressly; Mercans applies one twelfth.
Global WTC Coverage!A17	16.0
Global WTC Coverage!B17	Egypt F20 guards
Global WTC Coverage!C17	COMMISSION IS PRESENT as a first-class wage type (CO-COMISIONES) with its own nomina electronica element, and the global commission-bearing codes are configured, not dropped. EVERY tax code in column G is drawn from the Colombian legend below (CO-TX-01 to CO-TX-10 and CO-NA) and the validator rejects any code that does not start with 'CO-'. NO Yes-No appears in a taxability or subjection column without an explanation in column D.
Global WTC Coverage!A19	TAX CODE LEGEND (C9 - every code defined and sourced to a Colombian authority publication)
Global WTC Coverage!A20	Tax Code
Global WTC Coverage!B20	Meaning
Global WTC Coverage!C20	Official Source
Global WTC Coverage!A21	CO-TX-01
Global WTC Coverage!B21	Ordinary taxable work income - RESIDENT. Enters the total taxable payments of the month, is depurated under ET art. 388 (non-taxable income, art. 387 deductions, exempt income, the 40% cap and the 1,340-UVT annual cap) and is withheld on the ET art. 383 progressive table (0/19/28/33/35/37/39%) under Procedure 1 (art. 385) or at the fixed percentage under Procedure 2 (art. 386). No preferential rate and no tax-free allowance exists for any individual earning type.
Global WTC Coverage!C21	Estatuto Tributario (Decreto-Ley 624 de 1989) arts. 383 (table as replaced by Ley 2010 de 2019 art. 42), 385, 386, 387, 388; Decreto 1625 de 2016 art. 1.2.4.1.6 par. 3 (as modified by Decreto 2231 de 2023 art. 9); Resolucion DIAN 000238 de 15 diciembre 2025 art. 1 (UVT 52,374).
Global WTC Coverage!A22	CO-TX-02
Global WTC Coverage!B22	Severance savings and the interest on them (cesantias e intereses). EXCLUDED from the monthly withholding base under BOTH procedures - they never enter the monthly table read. Exempt IN FULL where the average monthly income of the last 6 months is <= 350 UVT (18,330,900); above that the untaxed portion tapers 90/80/60/40/20/0% through 650 UVT. The 25% exemption of art. 206 num. 10 does NOT apply to them (art. 206 par. 2).
Global WTC Coverage!C22	ET arts. 206 num. 4 and par. 2, 385, 386; Ley 50 de 1990 art. 99; Ley 52 de 1975 art. 1; Ley 2466 de 2025 art. 64 (8.33% monthly advance deposit).
Global WTC Coverage!A23	CO-TX-03
Global WTC Coverage!B23	Statutory minimum service bonus (prima de servicios legal). Under Procedure 1 it is withheld SEPARATELY, on its own read of the ET art. 383 table, and is NOT added to the month's salary. Under Procedure 2 it is folded into the ordinary monthly base (the 13 divisor of art. 386 already prorates its effect). Any excess above the statutory minimum bonus goes to the ordinary monthly base.
Global WTC Coverage!C23	ET arts. 385 and 386; CST arts. 306 (as amended by Ley 1788 de 2016 art. 1), 307 and 308.
Global WTC Coverage!A24	CO-TX-04
Global WTC Coverage!B24	Non-taxable income - ingreso no constitutivo de renta ni ganancia ocasional. Subtracted at step (B) of the ET art. 388 depuration, i.e. BEFORE the 40% cap reference figure is struck, which makes it more valuable than exempt income. Covers the employee mandatory pension 4%, the solidarity pension fund 1% + 0.2-1.0%, the mandatory health 4%, and voluntary contributions to the individual pension account within 25% of annual labour income / 2,500 UVT.
Global WTC Coverage!C24	ET arts. 55 and 56; ET art. 388 num. 1; Formato 2276 v4 numerals N23 and N24 (which group the pension and solidarity-fund contributions on a single line).
Global WTC Coverage!A25	CO-TX-05
Global WTC Coverage!B25	Conditionally not income - third-party meal payments (pagos de alimentacion a terceros). NOT income to the employee where BOTH conditions hold: the employee's salary does not exceed 310 UVT (16,235,940) AND the payment is made to a THIRD PARTY. Where the month's payments exceed 41 UVT (2,147,334) the EXCESS is taxable employment income. Cash paid to the employee for food is salary and gets none of this treatment.
Global WTC Coverage!C25	ET art. 387-1; Formato 2276 v4 numerals N08 (excess) and N32 (within-limit amount), which report the two portions separately; Formulario 220 casillas 37 and 38.
Global WTC Coverage!A26	CO-TX-06
Global WTC Coverage!B26	Not income and not remuneration - amounts given not to enrich the employee but to enable the work: statutory work clothing (dotacion), work tools, protective equipment, means of transport, representation expenses, occasional viaticos for transport, and genuine documented business-expense reimbursement. Outside EVERY base - tax, contribution, payroll-levy, statutory-benefit and vacation.
Global WTC Coverage!C26	CST arts. 128 and 230 (art. 230 as modified by Ley 11 de 1984 art. 7); CST arts. 130 and 232; ET art. 387-1 final paragraph (representation expenses expressly outside the meal rule).
Global WTC Coverage!A27	CO-TX-07
Global WTC Coverage!B27	Exempt income LIMITED TO THE STATUTORY MINIMUM AMOUNT - accident and illness indemnities, maternity-protection indemnities and employee burial expenses. ET art. 206 par. 1 is explicit that the exemption 'operates only on the amounts corresponding to the statutory minimum' and that the excess is not exempt. It does NOT extend to incapacity or maternity / paternity benefits, which are taxable (DIAN Oficio 27408 de 2019 items 7.1-7.4).
Global WTC Coverage!C27	ET art. 206 nums. 1, 2, 3 and par. 1; CST arts. 227, 236; Decreto 780 de 2016 art. 3.2.1.10 (Decreto 2943 de 2013 art. 1, employer days 1-2).
Global WTC Coverage!A28	CO-TX-08
Global WTC Coverage!B28	NON-RESIDENT flat withholding - 20% ON THE GROSS PAYMENT, with NO art. 388 depuration, NO 25% exempt income, NO art. 387 deductions and NO tax-free band, for Colombian-source work income. Determined at the moment of EACH payment on the status then held; the switch to the art. 383 table is PROSPECTIVE from the payment following the 183rd day, never retroactive. Where the service is performed entirely outside Colombia the income is foreign-source and there is NO withholding. NOTE: this code is a RESIDENCY-DRIVEN OVERLAY, applied per employee through column P (Special Tax) rather than assigned to any specific wage-type row -- zero rows carrying this code in the main catalogue is expected, not an omission (CO-QA-REPORT.md MINOR-09).
Global WTC Coverage!C28	ET arts. 10, 408-415; DIAN Concepto 011108 de 29 junio 2026 (upholding Concepto 1789 int. 14595 de 2025); DIAN Oficio 28380 de 2019; Formulario 350 casillas 109-112 and hoja 2 casillas 141-155.
Global WTC Coverage!A29	CO-TX-09
Global WTC Coverage!B29	Exempt income - renta exenta - for voluntary pension fund contributions (art. 126-1) and construction-savings / long-term-savings account contributions (arts. 126-4 AFC/AVC), employee or employer-paid on the employee's behalf. Subtracted at step (E) INSIDE the 40% and 1,340-UVT caps, within a single shared annual limit of the lower of 30% of labour income or 3,800 UVT (COP 199,021,200 for 2026).
Global WTC Coverage!C29	ET arts. 126-1 and 126-4; ET art. 388 num. 2; Formulario 220 casillas 56, 57 and 58; Formato 2276 v4 numerals N26, N27 and N28.
Global WTC Coverage!A30	CO-TX-10
Global WTC Coverage!B30	Pension income and refunds of pension savings. A retirement, invalidity, old-age, survivor or occupational-risk pension is taxable only on the monthly amount exceeding 1,000 UVT (COP 52,374,000). The 40% limit of art. 388 expressly does not apply to pension payments, pension substitution indemnities or refunds of pension savings. Paid by the pension administrator, so OUT OF SCOPE of employer gross-to-net per scope_manifest; retained for completeness and for Formulario 220 casilla 50 / Formato 2276 numeral N21.
Global WTC Coverage!C30	ET arts. 206 num. 5 and par. 3, 383 (first paragraph, which brings pensions into the same table), 388 final sentence; Formulario 220 casilla 50; Formato 2276 v4 numeral N21.
Global WTC Coverage!A31	CO-NA
Global WTC Coverage!B31	Not a remuneration line - an employee deduction, an employer contribution or accrual, a calculation base, an intermediary, a notional, a rate or a configuration flag. Every taxability and subjection column is NA because the row is a mechanism, not a payment to the employee.
Global WTC Coverage!C31	N/A - mechanism line. Reported, where reported at all, in the Deducciones block of the nomina electronica (Resolucion DIAN 000227 de 2025 arts. 1.5.3.1.1 to 1.5.3.9.8, Anexo Tecnico v1.0) or in the PILA settlement record (Resolucion MSPS 2388 de 2016 Anexo Tecnico 2).
